# Graph Report - autonomous-ai-agency  (2026-08-01)

## Corpus Check
- 1364 files · ~1,915,336 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 26676 nodes · 54021 edges · 1177 communities (1064 shown, 113 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 89% EXTRACTED · 11% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6050 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `043ca465`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
